1102.TT 亞泥 
 煤氣成本走揚收窄利差 短期展望保守

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
亞泥成立於 1927 年，核心業務為水泥、熟料的生產與銷售，市場以臺灣及中國大陸
為主公司，另公司也拓展電力與不鏽鋼領域。2026 年營收組成上，大陸水泥 32.5%、
台灣水泥 32.3%、電力 26.1%、不鏽鋼 3.5%、其他 5.5%。   
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
區間操作 37 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，38 
【大盤指數/股價】45857 / 33.45

➢ 2Q26 水泥需求下滑，惟轉投資挹注獲利 
2Q26 營收 178.3 億元，QoQ+20.24%，YoY-4.47%，中國水泥事業出
貨量仍較 2Q25 略微下滑 ， 且報價亦呈現疲軟走勢 ； 台灣水泥事業方面 ，
受到政府信用管制，導致國內新開工案量減少，影響建商開案進度，導
致國內水泥需求下滑；嘉惠電廠 因天然氣價格走揚，利差收窄，影響整
體 獲 利 表 現 。2Q26 毛 利 率 約 14.03% ， 稅 後 淨 利 52.27 億 元 ，
QoQ+176.98%，YoY+78.4%， 主要受惠轉投資獲利成長 ， 再加上2Q26
公司金融資產及負債評價利益增加所帶動，EPS 為 1.57 元。 
➢ 3Q26 煤、氣成本上揚，利差收窄，獲利表現較差 
展望 3Q26，中國大陸水泥部分 ， 預期3Q26 報價將與 2Q26 大致持平 ，
但受煤炭成本上升影響，預估噸毛利將較 2Q26 略微收斂。在台灣水泥
事業方面 ， 科技廠辦對預拌混凝土的穩健需求 ，2H26 預拌表現將持續優
於水泥。嘉惠電力 2H26 恐延續 2Q26 獲利表現趨勢，亦即利差收窄情
況 。 整體而言 ，3Q26 水泥與發電本業雖受制於煤 、 氣燃料成本高而面臨
利潤壓縮，但整體營運仍維持穩健，且透過多元布局及轉投資，分散產
業循環影響。投研部預估 3Q26 營收 169.76 億元，QoQ-4.79%，YoY-
5.1%，毛利率 12.85%，EPS 為 0.72 元，QoQ-51.07%，YoY-32.54%。 
➢ 2026 政策、地緣政治等仍具不確定性，展望相對保守 
預估 2026 年營收 674.45 億元，YoY-5.1%，毛利率 13.47%，稅後淨利
123.88 億元 ，YoY+23.5%，EPS 為 3.62 元 。 預估2027 年營收約 684.21
億元，YoY+1.45%，毛利率約 13.3%，稅後淨利為 108.85 億元，YoY-
12.14%，EPS 為 3.07 元。 
➢ 短期營運承壓，建議區間操作，目標價 37 元 
整體而言，考量中國水泥需求將呈現逐年緩降的趨勢，對於短期中國水
泥事業成長動能保守看待。台灣方面，2026 年政策底部雖已逐漸浮現，
惟房市回溫仍需時間，再加上電廠利差收窄影響獲利表現評價方面，
2026 年成長動能承壓。預估 2026 年淨值為 52.15 元，過往 PB 評價落
在 0.6X~1.25X 區間 ， 目前為0.64X，雖然位於區間下緣 ， 然考量營運多
空紛雜，維持區間操作建議，目標價 37 元(2026F PB 0.7X)。

公司簡介 
亞泥成立於 1927 年，核心業務為水泥、熟料的生產與銷售，市場以臺灣及中國大陸
為主。旗下亞泥中國的產品廣泛應用於基礎建設與房地產項目。公司同時拓展至電力
與不鏽鋼領域，嘉惠電力經營臺灣天然氣發電廠，透過與臺電的長期購電合約確保穩
定運營，遠龍不鏽鋼則專注於高品質不鏽鋼製造，產品應用於建築與工業領域，兩者
有效提升整體盈利能力。2026 年營收組成上，大陸水泥 32.5%、台灣水泥 32.3%、
電力 26.1%、不鏽鋼 3.5%、其他 5.5%。 
圖 1：亞泥營收組合

資料來源：國票投顧整理 
2Q26 水泥需求下滑，惟轉投資挹注獲利 
2Q26 營收 178.3 億元，QoQ+20.24%，YoY-4.47%，由各事業部門觀察，亞泥中國
事業方面，由於中國內需仍相對疲弱，中國水泥事業出貨量仍較 2Q25 略微下滑，且
報價亦呈現疲軟走勢，拖累整體營收表現；台灣水泥事業方面，受到政府信用管制，
導致國內新開工案量減少，加上年初土方之亂，影響建商開案進度，導致國內水泥需
求下滑，拖累該事業群營收表現；嘉惠電廠則進入發電旺季，營收季增逾50%，惟天
然氣價格走揚，嘉惠一期在轉嫁氣價的能力相對較差，導致利差收窄，影響整體獲利
表現。2Q26 毛利率約 14.03%，QoQ+1.02ppts，YoY-4.04ppts，雖然電廠毛利率
下滑，然而受惠於 1Q26 煤炭價格位處低檔，其成本優勢反映在 2Q26 獲利表現上，
故 2Q26 亞泥中國事業噸毛利仍較 1Q26 成長。 2Q26 稅後淨利 52.27 億元，
QoQ+176.98%，YoY+78.4%，主要受惠於轉投資遠東新(1402)及裕民(2606)航運獲
利成長，再加上 2Q26 公司金融資產及負債評價利益增加所帶動，EPS 為 1.57 元。 
3Q26 煤、氣成本上揚，利差收窄，獲利表現較差 
展望 3Q26，水泥事業方面雖然進入產業淡季，依地區來看，在中國大陸水泥部分，
預期 3Q26 報價將與 2Q26 大致持平，主要受惠四川地區拉動，但受煤炭成本上升影
響，預估 3Q26 噸毛利將較 2Q26 略微收斂，此外，黃岡二線若進展順利，預計於
3Q26 投產，可望帶動出貨量。在台灣水泥事業方面，雖 1H26 整體需求衰退 15%，
但隨著信用管制放寬等房市政策底部浮現，加上科技廠辦對預拌混凝土的穩健需求，
2H26 預拌表現將持續優於水泥。而電力事業上，受制於美伊僵局及荷姆茲海峽封鎖
導致天然氣價格上漲，嘉惠電力 2H26 恐延續 2Q26 獲利表現趨勢，亦即利差收窄情

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
況。整體而言，3Q26 水泥與發電本業雖受制於煤、氣燃料成本高而面臨利潤壓縮，
但整體營運仍維持穩健，且透過多元布局及轉投資，分散產業循環影響。投研部預估
3Q26 營收 169.76 億元 ，QoQ-4.79%，YoY-5.1%， 毛利率12.85%，QoQ-1.19ppts，
YoY-3.84ppts， 稅後淨利25.58 億元 ，QoQ-51.07%，YoY-32.54%，EPS 為 0.72 元。 
圖 2：煤炭價格表現相對強勢，不利 3Q26 利差表現

資料來源：WIND, 國票投顧整理 
2026 政策、地緣政治等仍具不確定性，展望相對保守 
展望 2026 年，中國水泥事業方面，雖然整體房市仍較為疲弱，預計仍將導致需求下
滑，然而觀察近期部份一線城市放寬限購限貸，以及三條紅線監管淡化，將有助於限
縮水泥需求跌幅，並且中期而言，中國十五五計畫強化科技自主、國防安全，預期基
礎建設計畫將有結構調整，有助改善產業朝高質化水泥發展。供給面除了前述產能置
換的政策外，短期仍以錯峰生產為最有效限制供給的手段，惟其高度仰賴企業自律，
具一定不確定性。 
整體而言，預期亞泥中國受惠於產能置換，將有助於提高生產效率及能耗，同時由於
產線自湖北轉移自黃岡，其距離較礦山近，也有利於成本上的降低，此外，較高毛利
的骨料也受惠於產能去瓶頸，2026 年產能可望進一步提升並貢獻營收，惟整體出貨
量預估受到產業環境影響，預估將較 2025 年下滑。 
此外，於獲利表現方面，由於近期煤價表現相對強勁，主要受到中東戰爭導致油氣供
給減少，部分燃料需求轉向煤炭，再加上先前山西煤礦意外，中國政府加強安檢，導
致供應收緊，故預期短期煤價不易下滑，全年噸毛利約落在 15 元人民幣。 
台灣水泥事業方面 ， 仍需觀察政府後續政策實施，2026 年因房市信用管制 ， 資金收縮
影響建商購地及開工意願，導致 1H26 水泥需求量下滑，展望後市，考量政策底部已
經慢慢浮現，預期信用管制不易再度收窄，包含信用管制亦已部分條件放寬，加上新
青安政策延續 ， 故預期房市最懷情況已過 ， 隨剛性需求逐漸進場 ， 將有助2H26、2027
年建商開案數量增加，進一步帶動整體水泥需求。另一方面，政府已對越南進口水泥
課徵反傾銷稅，未來亦有望針對印尼、日本提出反傾銷調查，加上廠辦需求，亦有助

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
於台灣水泥市場供需持穩，故有望看到水泥需求緩步回溫；最後在天然氣方面，由於
台灣有部分天然氣經由荷姆茲海峽運送，故天然氣成本勢必 在地緣政治影響下提高，
使嘉惠電廠利差收窄，不利於獲利表現。 
綜上所述，2026 年仍受到政策、地緣政治等不確定性影響，營收成長動能相對有限，
預估 2026 年營收 674.45 億元，YoY-5.1%，毛利率 13.47%，稅後淨利 123.88 億
元，YoY+23.5%，EPS 為 3.62 元。預估 2027 年營收約 684.21 億元，YoY+1.45%，
毛利率約 13.3%，稅後淨利為 108.85 億元，YoY-12.14%，EPS 為 3.07 元。 
短期營運承壓，建議區間操作，目標價 37 元 
整體而言 ， 雖然中國政府嚴格執行反內捲政策 ， 以及各家廠商進行錯峰生產嚴控產量 ，
惟錯峰生產仍需仰賴企業自律行為，並且考量中國水泥需求將呈現逐年緩降的趨勢，
對於短期中國水泥事業成長動能保守看待。 
台灣方面 ，2026 年政策底部雖已逐漸浮現 ， 為房市回溫仍需時間 ， 短期房市交易仍相
對冷淡，再加上電廠利差收窄影響獲利表現 評價方面，2026 年成長動能承壓。預估
2026 年淨值為 52.15 元，過往 PB 評價落在 0.6X~1.25X 區間，目前為 0.64X，雖然
位於區間下緣，然考量營運多空紛雜，維持區間操作建議，目標價 37 元(2026F PB 
0.7X)。

## Document 004: 1102亞泥 第一金

### Page 1

羅子評 
(886-2)2563-1666 
jason.luo@firstsec.com.tw

亞泥(1102) 
區間操作, TP 37 (潛在報酬 10.61%)  
區間操作 
Price(08/17)：33.45 
Target Price：37

亞泥 26Q2 單季營收達 178.31 億元 ，QoQ 20.24%，YoY -4.47%。
雖受中國煤炭成本攀升及報價低迷影響，拖累合併毛利率降至
14.03%；然受惠遠東新與裕民等業外轉投資挹注，單季EPS 仍逆
勢跳增至 1.57 元。展望後市，考量中國水泥市場呈 L 型築底且台
灣靜待房市鬆綁，本業延續承壓基調。研究部預估2026 全年 EPS
為 3.27 元；若以過往 65%配息率推估，2027 年有望配發 2.13 元
現金股利，配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。 
評價方面， 考量整體水泥產業仍處於下行階段 ， 且本業營收與獲利
持續承壓，給予過往區間中緣偏下之 0.7 倍 PBR 評價，目標價設
定為 37 元。惟業外獲利強勁有效彌補本業缺口，並提供配息下檔
保護；鑑於公司具備長期殖利率保護優勢，投資建議維持區間操
作，建議可逢低布局。  
⚫ 近期投資評等 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
亞洲水泥隸屬於遠東集團 ， 為台灣與中國具指標性之建材與多
角化控股公司。集團主要營運版圖涵蓋水泥、非水泥及策略轉
投資三大板塊。以近期營收結構分析，核心水泥本業合計貢獻
總營收將近七成，其中台灣市場約占近四成，中國市場約占三
成；非水泥事業則以電力領域為營運第二隻腳，穩定貢獻約兩
成營收，其餘則為不鏽鋼及其他業務。此外，亞泥亦透過權益
法戰略投資遠東新、裕民及山水水泥等關聯企業，建構出具防
禦性且多角化的穩健獲利基礎。

⚫ 中國水泥事業低迷，惟業外貢獻支撐 26Q2 EPS 至 1.57 元 
26Q2 工作天數增加，台灣水泥出貨季增；但中國市場報價偏
低且需求年減，使集團單季營收達 178.31 億元， QoQ 
20.24%，YoY -4.47%。獲利方面，因中國煤炭成本攀升與終
端報價低迷，中國事業噸毛利由 25Q2 的 40 元人民幣降至約
15 元 ， 並轉為虧損 ， 拖累合併毛利率降至14.03%， 年減4.04
個百分點，季增 1.02 個百分點，營業利益 17.11 億元，QoQ 
44.52%，YoY -34.09%。惟業外受惠遠東新與裕民轉投資支
撐，彌補本業缺口，其中權益法投資認列13.95 億元，金融資
產評價利益 26.58 億元。單季稅後獲利跳增至 52.27 億元，
QoQ 176.98%，YoY 78.40%，EPS 達 1.57 元。

2 
 ⚫ 本業展望保守，惟業外貢獻支撐配息下檔保護 
展望後市，隨中國房市未見起色，台灣建築市場仍以存量消耗
為主，亞泥下半年整體營運將延續本業築底、轉投資彌補整體
獲利之基調。預期公司全年 EPS 為 3.27 元，按照過往 65%
配息率推估，預估 2027 可配發 2.13 元現金股利。

水泥：中國 L 型築底，台灣靜待房市鬆綁 
中國市場受房地產需求量下行與市場報價維持低位，需求呈L
型復甦。亞泥中國採取保市佔策略，預估全年出貨量約下滑
2%， 幅度低於中國平均約10%衰退 。 展望下半年 ， 預期26Q3
報價持平，但受煤炭成本維持高位盤整，預期26Q3 噸毛利恐
略低於 26Q2，全年噸毛利預估落於 15 元人民幣附近。台灣
市場上半年受房市信用管制及土方新制干擾，需求量下滑約
15%。然台灣水泥報價維持平穩，且預拌混凝土事業受惠於科
技廠辦需求支撐，毛利率表現相對穩健。下半年隨政策底部逐
漸浮現與剛性買盤進場，後續將持續觀察開工與施工進度。

資料來源:亞洲水泥 26Q2 法說、第一金投顧匯整

非水泥事業：嘉惠具基本盤，不鏽鋼維持損平 
嘉惠電力下半年營運趨勢預期與 26Q2 相當 ， 主要受國際天然
氣價格走揚使成本承壓，預期營利呈小幅年減。但受惠與台電
的保證發電量合約機制，嘉惠仍具獲利下檔支撐，後續成長空
間端視氣價與台電調度、增購需求而定。遠龍不鏽鋼事業則因
終端市況偏弱，加上海外原料採購及庫存調節具波動性，預期
下半年獲利將維持於損益兩平線波動。 
業外貢獻支撐，預估 2026 全年 EPS 為 3.27 元 
綜上所述，儘管短期兩岸水泥本業面臨逆風，但業外貢獻仍對
合併獲利提供支撐，研究部預估 2026 亞泥全年營收 673.20
億，YoY -5.23%；毛利率 13.67%，年減 2.16 個百分點；
營業利益 61.14 億，YoY -27.27%； 稅後獲利 111.42 億，YoY 
11.09%；EPS 為 3.27 元；配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。

雖受中國煤炭成本攀升及報價低迷影響，拖累合併毛利率降至
14.03%；然受惠遠東新與裕民等業外轉投資強勢挹注，單季 EPS
仍逆勢跳增至 1.57 元 。 展望後市 ， 考量中國水泥市場呈L 型築底且
台灣靜待房市鬆綁 ， 本業延續承壓基調 。 研究部預估2026 全年 EPS
為 3.27 元；若以過往 65%配息率推估，2027 年有望配發 2.13 元
現金股利，配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。

### Page 1

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
2Q26 Beat Reinforce AI Thermal Ramp; Raised TP to NT$3,800
CITI'S TAKE 
AVC delivered a solid 2Q26 beat, with reported net profit of NT$9.6bn 
topping Citi/cons estimates by 11%/12% respectively, driven by margin 
outperformance and non-ops boost. GPM improvement was above 
expectations on continued mix shift toward server and liquid cooling 
solutions. Mgmt. ’s guidance implies sequential growth led by AI ASIC ramp-
up, with Vera Rubin generation platform contribution expected by quarter-
end. We model 16% QoQ revenue growth for 3Q26E, accelerating further 
into 4Q26E as GPU and AI ASIC liquid cooling ramps. Reaffirmed 
2026E/27E capex also underscores confidence in capacity expansion, in our 
view. We raise our 2026-28E earnings by 5%/12%/18% to reflect updated 
guidance and constructive 2H26E outlook and lift our TP to NT$3,800 from 
NT$3,450 (25x 2027E EPS). Reiterate Buy.
2Q26 results beat on margins & non-ops — AVC’s reported 2Q26 net profit of 
NT$9.6bn (+21% QoQ/+139% Y o Y) beat Citi/cons forecast by 11%/12%, driven by 
better-than-expected margins and non-ops. 2Q26 GPM came in at 32.6% (+2.8ppts 
QoQ/+8.2ppts Y o Y), above both Citi/cons estimates, mainly due to further mix 
improvement and a higher server revenue contribution. Non-ops included NT$255m of 
net interest income, providing an incremental boost to the bottom.
Broadening AI ASIC ramp and GPU transition support 2H26E acceleration — 
Mgmt. guided for revenue growth in 2H26 compared to 1H26, primarily driven by 
mass production ramp-up of AI ASIC project, with the Vera Rubin generation 
platform expected to see contribution by quarter-end. We now model 16% QoQ 
revenue growth for 3Q26E, and expect further acceleration in 4Q26E as GPU and AI 
ASIC liquid cooling products ramp simultaneously. GPM guidance also points to 
sequential improvement vs. 2Q26, driven by a higher server/liquid cooling mix and 
stronger operating leverage. Mgmt. reaffirmed 2026E/27E capex guidance of 
NT$15bn/15+bn, reiterating confidence in ongoing capacity expansion.
Implications — We raise our 2026E/27E/28E earnings forecasts by 5%/12%/18% to 
reflect updated guidance and constructive 2H26E outlook. We lift our TP to NT$3,800, 
still based on 25x 2027E EPS. We believe AVC’s solid supply position across key 
customers’ key AI GPU and AI ASIC projects, combined with a sustained revenue and 
margin growth trajectory, could continue to support the stock price strength. With the 
platform transition risk now appearing largely resolved and 2H26E visibility improving, 
we see continued upside to consensus estimates. We reiterate our Buy rating.

Bull/Bear: Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)

### Page 6

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
12 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Asia Vital Components
Company description
Asia Vital Components (AVC), founded in 1991, is a global leader in thermal
solutions, originally focusing on PCs but now specializing in servers and
networking. With Furukawa Electric (Japan) as its largest shareholder, AVC
provides a comprehensive range of cooling components, including vapor
chambers (VCs), fans, cold plates, CDUs, and manifolds, as well as chassis

and assembly services for PCs and servers, enabling holistic thermal solution
designs and one-stop shop services. AVC's diverse product portfolio supports
various sectors, including consumer IT, cloud infrastructure, transportation,
energy and networking.
Investment strategy
We rate AVC a Buy. AVC is well-positioned for the liquid-cooling transition,
with leadership in cold plates and broad participation across Nvidia and CSP
projects. Its high-volume machining capability, manufacturing uniformity,

and Vietnam-based capacity provide it with a defensible postion as Blackwell
and future deployments expand. The company captures a wider share of the
thermal BOM, across coldplates, manifolds, QDs, and CDU opportunities,
providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$3,800 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified

given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,
rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-

plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not
been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

Key highlights of the 2Q26 earnings call
2Q result beat on better OPM
Hon Hai delivered another record quarter, with 2Q26 revenue of NT$2.53tn (up
19% QoQ, 41% YoY) and OPM at 3.75% (vs 3.6% in 1Q26, beat consensus of 3.3%).
Management expects YoY OPM expansion for the year while cloud/networking
exceeded 50% of revenue for the first time in 2Q26. 3Q revenue is guided to grow
significantly QoQ/YoY, driven by continued AI growth plus the traditional peak

season.

AI Rack the Key Incremental Driver
Smart Consumer Electronics should grow significantly QoQ and YoY, while
Computing Products are expected to decline slightly QoQ as the new-product peak
passes and memory supply remains tight, but still grow significantly YoY.

Components & Others should also post significant YoY growth. Overall, AI Rack’s
will be the key incremental driver and management expects 3Q AI rack shipments
to grow high-double-digits QoQ as existing platforms continue ramping and next-
generation products begin production.

Figure 4. Hon Hai - 2026E Business Outlook
Source: Company Reports
Networking Becomes the Next AI Growth Engine
Hon Hai sees networking and optical connectivity as the next major growth pillar after
AI compute. Networking revenue delivered strong growth in 1H26, driven by rising AI
cluster scale and continued upgrades in switch bandwidth. Its higher-end switch
products remain on schedule, with new-generation switches already shipping primarily

for scale-out connectivity between AI racks, while the product roadmap should expand
alongside future system architectures. More importantly, Hon Hai is moving beyond
switch assembly into a vertically integrated networking platform encompassing
optical transceivers, cables, connectors, high-speed interconnects, power modules
and advanced packaging. Management believes optical communications could
eventually represent a US$100bn+ addressable market, making networking an

increasingly meaningful contributor to both revenue and profitability. As AI clusters
become larger and more interconnected, the networking content per AI system should
rise structurally faster than server unit growth.
ASIC Expands Rapidly — Revenue Understates the Real
Business Scale
ASIC AI servers are emerging as a second major growth engine alongside Hon Hai’s
dominant GPU franchise. Management said the company has secured new ASIC
customers and projects in 2026, and expects its ASIC market share to increase

significantly, with a longer-term target of 40%+. Hon Hai’s differentiation is its
ability to participate across multiple layers—from modules and cooling systems to
Compute Trays, rack integration and complete system architecture, particularly for
increasingly complex liquid-cooled and high-power ASIC systems. ASIC
represented roughly 10% of AI-server revenue in 2025, with the 1H26
contribution slightly higher.  With new products ramping in 2H26, Hon Hai

expects both ASIC shipment scale and profit contribution to continue increasing.

### Page 6

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Figure 5. Hon Hai - AI Server Business Outlook
Source: Company Reports
Consignment vs. Buy-and-Sell: Lower Revenue, Better
Capital Efficiency
Compared to GPU-based project, ASIC projects currently have a significantly
higher consignment mix because system architecture and customer requirements
are more customized. Management expects consignment penetration to gradually
rise as ASIC projects expand, and GPU projects could also adopt consignment

depending on customer requirements. Financially, this reduces inventory exposure,
component-price risk and working-capital requirements while improving asset
turnover. With potentially increasing consignment business model thanks to rising
ASIC exposure and better OPM sustainability, we are positive on Hon Hai’s
earnings growth trajectory.
Capacity Expansion Accelerates Ahead of the Next AI Ramp
Hon Hai is aggressively expanding capacity ahead of the next AI product cycle.

Following capex of approximately NT$136.3bn in 2024 and NT$173.8bn in 2025,
management expects 2026 capex to increase by more than 30% YoY, primarily to
expand server, AI rack, liquid-cooling and testing capacity while strengthening
global manufacturing and automation. Expansion is underway across Taiwan, the
U.S., Mexico and Vietnam, with U.S. R&D/manufacturing capacity specifically
increasing in Texas, Wisconsin and California to support localization requirements.

Although rapid growth pushed 1H26 free cash flow to a NT$150bn outflow due to
higher working capital and NT$80.9bn of capex, underlying cash generation
remains strong: adjusted EBITDA increased 52% YoY, or NT$77.6bn, significantly
exceeding the increase in capex and interest expense. Management therefore
views current investment as growth-driven rather than defensive—the

### Page 7

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 August 2026 Citi Research
7
combination of higher CSP capex, rising Hon Hai market share, vertical integration
and automation should translate the capacity build-out into stronger revenue,
profitability and capital efficiency over the coming years.
Reiterate Buy with TP NT$400
Thanks to better order visibility, robust OPM trend and more value upgrade for
next-generation AI infrastructure, we lift our 2026/2027 /2028 earnings estimates

by 8%/3%/8% for Hon Hai reflecting better order visibility and its advantage of
operational scale, vertical integration, automation and efficiency. We move our
target from 2026 to now 2026/2027 average, based on 18x PER, from prior 20x,
reflecting recent market concern on macro uncertainties.

Bull/Bear: Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)

### Page 10

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 August 2026 Citi Research
10
Hon Hai Precision
Company description
Established in 1974, Hon Hai has become the largest EMS player in the world
in terms of sales. Through its vertical integration model or "eCMMS" (e-
enabled Components, Modules, Moves and Services), it provides
components, modules and system assembly products to many global OEMs
in the PC, communication and consumer sectors.
Investment strategy
We rate Hon Hai shares as Buy as we believe the company’s margins will

continue to improve, backed by a renewed focus on profitability, operational
efficiency and new businesses development. We think there’s more upside to
go from current level, as the underlying drivers are not just about product
cycle, but also about fundamental transformation and new business
opportunities.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 18x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is
at the higher end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is

justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook,
as well as new endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon
Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe
a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

## Document 075: 260812_daiwa_avc

Source PDF: all report pdf/260812_daiwa_avc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: AVC announced its 2Q26 results and hosted an investor 
conference on 12 August 2026 after market hours. We believe AVC should 
be one of the key beneficiaries in terms of a growing TAM for thermal 
solutions with an aggressive capacity expansion plan over 2026-28. We 
reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest memo, 2Q26 revenue roughly in 
line; bright 2H26 and 2027 outlook, on 7 July 2026).

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat. AVC’s 2Q26 revenue of 
TWD49,121m (+0.2 QoQ and +66.0% YoY) was roughly in line with our and 
Bloomberg consensus estimates. Its 2Q26 gross margin came in at 32.6% 
(vs. 29.8% in 1Q26 and 24.4% in 2Q25), a record-high level, higher than 
our and the consensus estimates of 31.0% on a better product mix and 
rising server revenue contribution of 66% in 2Q26 (vs. 66% in 1Q26 and 
51% in 2Q25). Its operating profit of TWD13,478m (+12.1% QoQ and 
+164.5% YoY) beat our and consensus estimates by 6.7% and 6.6%, 
respectively, thanks to better-than-expected operating efficiency. Thus, its 
operating margin of 27.4% (vs. 24.5% in 1Q26 and 17.2% in 2Q25), also a 
record-high level, beat our estimate (25.5%) and consensus (25.2%). With 
some non-op gains such as interest and FX and a lower-than-expected tax 
rate, its net profit of TWD9,567m (+20.9% QoQ and +139.3% YoY) beat our 
and consensus estimates by 11.9% and 10.5%, respectively. Its 2Q26 EPS 
finished at TWD24.37.

Key highlights from the investor conference – AI server revenue 
continue to rise. AVC is confident of maintaining its leading position as an 
integrator of thermal and mechanism solution and aims to be a primary 
supplier to ASICs and GPU clients. In 2027, the revenue from ASICs 
projects (with rising liquid cooling penetration, ie. 50%+ in 2027) should be 
higher than from GPU ones. AVC is working on LPU, CPO (multiple clients) 
and Rubin Ultra and aims for higher capex in 2027 (mainly in Vietnam), up 
from TWD15bn in 2026. Its 2H26 outlook should be better than that for 
1H26. We raised our 2026-28E EPS by 11-17%, mainly on our more 
aggressive revenue, gross margin and operating margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD3,880 (from TWD3,155), still based on a target PER of 29x vs. a 
past-3-year PER range of 5-26x [average PER of 15x]) applied to our 1-
year forward EPS. Downside risks: slower-than-expected progress in high-
gross-margin orders, slow AI server adoption, and competition.

How we differ: Our 2026E and 2028E EPS are 7%/12% above consensus, 
likely as we are relatively more aggressive in our gross margin and 
operating margin assumptions.

12 August 2026

2Q26 results review: improving margins profile

➢ 2Q26 EPS beat our and Bloomberg consensus estimates by 11-12% 
➢ Leading position unchanged from its thermal and mechanical specialty 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD3,880

Company profile 
AVC is a global leading comprehensive thermal solutions provider in segments related to desktops, 
laptops, communication, servers, energy and autos. Its main product line includes thermal products 
(thermal modules and fans), chassis, system assembly and peripheral products and hinges. The 
company has been listed on Taiwan’s main board since September 2002.

## Document 078: 260812_daiwa_lotes

Source PDF: all report pdf/260812_daiwa_lotes.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Lotes released its 2Q26 results after market hours on 12 August. Lotes' 2Q26 
earnings came in at TWD2.0bn (EPS: TWD17.6), which was 30% lower than our and 17% 
lower than the street's expectations. While revenue was slightly lower than expected, 2Q26 
gross margin decreased by 4.0pp QoQ to 45.5%, driven mainly by rising raw material costs 
(eg, copper, gold, plastics) and a delayed margin uplift from new-generation CPU platforms 
due to component shortages in the supply chain. Operating margin declined 7.0pp QoQ to 
22.0%, reflecting higher-than-expected opex from a one-time employee stock bonus 
expense. Looking into 3Q26, we expect top-line momentum to remain robust, driven 
mainly by strength in Lotes' QD business. However, while QD growth supports the top line, 
it also weighs on margins given its lower profitability profile. Combined with elevated raw 
material costs and an unfavourable product mix shift, we believe margin pressure will 
persist into 3Q26. We think a meaningful improvement in profitability will require higher 
penetration of next-generation CPU platforms, which should provide a more substantial 
margin boost once ramped. That said, despite these near-term headwinds, we remain 
positive on Lotes given its long-term positioning as a key CPU server play. Lotes is hosting 
results call on 13 August and we will provide updates in due course. We have a Buy (1) 
rating on Lotes with a 12M TP of TWD3,533, based on a target PER of 28x applied to our 
1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key risk: lower-than-expected adoption of server CPU 
platforms.

Lotes’ 2Q26 results highlights

What’s the impact: 
Below are our read-throughs from Lotes’ 2Q26 results. Lotes is hosting results call on 13 
August and we will provide updates in due course.

• 2Q26 results dragged by gross margin and high opex. Lotes’ 2Q26 earnings came 
in at TWD2.0bn (EPS: TWD17.6), which was 30% lower than our and 17% lower than 
the street’s expectations. While revenue was slightly lower than expected, 2Q26 gross 
margin decreased by 4.0pp QoQ to 45.5%, driven mainly by rising raw material costs 
(eg, copper, gold, plastics) and a delayed margin uplift from new-generation CPU 
platforms due to component shortages in the supply chain. Operating margin declined 
7.0pp QoQ to 22.0%, reflecting higher-than-expected opex from a one-time employee 
stock bonus expense. As for net profit, it was boosted by a higher-than-expected non-op 
gain of c.TWD500m (vs. our estimate of c.TWD120m).

Lotes 3533 TT 1,910 Buy 3.4 9% 19% 11.7 23% 38.73% 29% 17.7 13.0 8-21x 7-Aug 11.0 31% 
Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Lotes: monthly revenue trend and YoY growth

Source: Company

• Business outlook. Lotes reported July sales at TWD3.4bn (up 9% MoM and 19% YoY), 
which achieved 29% of our 3Q26 sales forecast (TWD11.7bn; up 23% QoQ and 39% 
YoY) and 31% of the Bloomberg consensus of TWD11.0bn. With July revenue 
accounting for 31-34% of Lotes’s 3Q revenue over the past five years, the run-rate is 
largely on track with the street’s expectation. Looking into 3Q26, we expect top-line 
momentum to remain robust, driven mainly by strength in Lotes' QD business. However, 
while QD growth supports the top line, it also weighs on margins given its lower 
profitability profile. Combined with elevated raw material costs and an unfavourable 
product mix shift, we believe margin pressure will persist into 3Q26. We think a 
meaningful improvement in profitability will require higher penetration of next-generation 
CPU platforms, which should provide a more substantial margin boost once ramped. 
That said, despite these near-term headwinds, we remain positive on Lotes given its 
long-term positioning as a key CPU server play. Lastly, Lotes announced an issuance of 
new shares (dilution: c.1.15%) and a TWD3bn convertible bond issuance (dilution: 
c.1.34%). Lotes is hosting results call on 13 August and we will provide updates in due 
course.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Lotes with a 12M TP of TWD3,533, based on a target PER of 
28x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key risk: lower-than-expected adoption of 
server CPU platforms.

## Document 079: 260812_daiwa_m31

Source PDF: all report pdf/260812_daiwa_m31.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: M31 reported a 2Q26 result that missed both our and consensus estimates, 
mainly due to delayed license project recognition, which led to scale diseconomies and a 
quarterly loss. Yet management remains optimistic that the delayed revenue can be 
recognized in 2H26 and thus reiterates its double-digit growth rate outlook for 2026. While 
we see downside risk to our current forecast trajectory, we are positive on M31’s structural 
business outlook. We have a Buy (1) rating on the stock with a 12M TP of TWD750.

Highlight 
• 2Q26 results missed on scale. M31 reported 2Q26 net loss of TWD3m, vs. 
our/consensus net profit estimates of TWD93m/TWD68m. We attribute the miss to 
lower-than-expected revenue, which came in at just TWD422m, missing our/consensus 
estimates by 20%/16% for scale diseconomies unable to cover opex of around 
TWD420-440m per quarter. M31 failed to recognise delayed license projects which it 
had expected at its results call last quarter: that the April revenue uptick should set a 
good stage for its monthly run-rates into May-June (Structural turnaround round the 
corner, 6 May). Management indicated that projects below 16nm have longer timelines, 
and some high-value projects are now recognized as licensing fees over five phases 
instead of three. Together, these factors lengthen the recognition period and mute near-
term revenue growth. Management reiterated a “cautiously optimistic” view that the 
delayed revenue can be recognized in 2H26 and the company could achieve its 2026 
outlook for double-digit YoY growth rate. That said, we see downside risk to our current 
forecast trajectory and will be reviewing our model in due course.

• Projects delayed, not derailed. While near-term revenue recognition came in below 
street expectations, the underlying growth drivers remain intact. High-speed interface 
functional IP and Foundation IP remain key focus areas, with PCIe, USB, UFS, MIPI 
and SerDes IPs supporting ongoing demand for high-speed transmission and image 
processing. M31 expects its PCIe Gen 6 project is to tape out in August, with planned 
development of 112G SerDes in 2H26. Overall, M31’s pipeline across mature and 
advanced process nodes, deep collaboration with main foundry partners, along with 
demand from high-end consumer, edge and cloud AI, AIoT and automotive applications, 
should continue to support its structural growth outlook.

Recommendation 
We rate M31 Buy (1) with a 12M TP of TWD750, based on our 4-quarter forward PER 
target of 45x. The stock has tumbled c.40% off its recent peak in May, introducing an 
attractive risk/reward profile on valuation grounds, in our opinion. Key downside risk to our 
current rating would be any geopolitical overhang (war, FX, interest rate) weighing on end-
demand and delaying M31’s royalty revenue ramps.

## Document 085: 260812_ml_GCE

Source PDF: all report pdf/260812_ml_GCE.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Gold Circuit

2Q26 lighter GM/OPM; still intact sales/GM 
growth from 3Q26; new PO NT$1,500, Buy

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,500 TWD | Price: 964.00 TWD

Lighter GM/OPM in 2Q: higher material/depreciation costs 
Gold Circuit’s (GCE) 2Q26 OP was 3% above BofAe but 2% below consensus. GM/OPM 
of 34.7%/26.4% came in below BofAe of 35.8%/27.4% and consensus of 36.0%/28.1%. 
We believe the margin outcome reflected larger sales scale (+26% QoQ, +75% YoY; 
7%/5% above BofAe/consensus), still favorable mix (server/networking accounted for 
78%/15% of 2Q26 sales, vs. 72%/20% in 1Q26 and 74%/13% in 2Q25), and higher 
material/depreciation costs. NP was 13%/8% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3), thanks 
to a lower tax rate, despite a non-operating loss (details to be disclosed), 
Still intact sales/GM growth from 3Q but HDI likely for T4 
We now expect 3Q/4Q26 sales +28%/+1% QoQ (taking 2026E sales +77% YoY) and 
2027/28E sales +37%/+26% YoY, driven by new ASIC platform launches, ASP growth 
from server spec upgrades, and ongoing capacity expansion in Taiwan/Thailand/China. 
We also expect GM to increase QoQ from 3Q26 as higher material costs are passed 
through. Looking ahead to AWS Trainium 4 generation, we believe GCE will remain a key 
AWS supplier. However, our latest check suggests that HDI technology will likely be used 
for Trainium 4, vs. GCE’s current focus on multi-layer board products. We thus adopt 
more conservative GM assumptions to reflect 1) potentially higher upfront/production 
costs to make HDI boards and 2) higher depreciation costs from building more new sites.  
Lower PO to NT$1,500 but reiterate Buy 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 6-9% mainly to reflect 2Q26 results, still solid sales/margin 
growth riding on the AI upcycle, and a lower tax rate (29% guidance vs. 32% earlier). We 
trim PO to NT$1,500 (25x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,810 (32x 2027E EPS), reflecting an 
unchanged valuation base but lower P/E multiple. We use 25x (2.5SD above P/E average 
since 2020, vs. prior 32x historical high) on slow margin expansion pace. Nevertheless, 
we continue to like GCE as a top ASIC server PCB maker amid strong AI demand, all-time 
focus on high-end AI server/networking PCBs, expanding margins. Reiterate Buy.

Key Metrics

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
Gold Circuit (GCE) is a PCB company in Taiwan with high 
exposure in server (74% of 2025 sales) and networking 
(15%). Key end-clients are global CSPs and US top 
networking/telecom names. Besides its headquarters, the 
company has production sites in Taoyuan (Taiwan), Suzhou 
(China), Jiangsu (China), and Thailand, with ongoing capacity 
expansion. GCE was established in 1981 and listed on the 
Taiwan stock market in March 1998.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Gold Circuit, given (1) its high 
exposure and focus on better-margin server/networking 
business amid rising AI applications globally to keep high 
ASP/margin, (2) strong competitive advantages and high 
entry barrier as focusing on high-end products, (3) higher 
production flexibility after adding capacity in Thailand, (4) 
positive FCF/OCF since 2007/2019, respectively, with an 
improving balance sheet (from net debt to net cash) and 
expanding ROE.

Price to Book Value 10.2x

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Gold Circuit (GOCCF) 
Our PO of NT$1,500 is based on 25x 2027E EPS. Our 25x valuation multiple (2.5SD 
above historical average since 2020 when 50%+ of sales from server) is similar to the 
average of its closest AI PCB peers' P/E multiple targets we set given (1) strong AI PCB 
market demand, (2) GCE's high AI server exposure, (3) solid sales mix from better-margin 
server/networking business, and (4) ongoing margin expansion to historical high level.

Upside risks: (1) stronger server/networking market demand to drive ASP/margins, (2) 
better utilization rate, (3) faster capacity expansion in Thailand, (4) lower raw 
material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) favorable FX movement.

Downside risks: (1) ASIC platform delay, (2) weaker market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) softer AI server demand due to AI chip short supply, (4) GM pressure 
from lower utilization rate, higher raw material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) unfavorable 
FX movement.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lenovo (0992.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (based
on a beta of 1.1, risk-free rate of 2.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Stronger PC demand recovery in China
n Better cost savings and improved product mix

n Lower-than-expected increase in memory price
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Weaker PC demand recovery in China
n Deterioration in cost savings and product mix
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory price
Acer Inc. (2353.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We use RI model for Acer's valuation. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 0.9, equity risk premium of 10.2% and risk-free rate

of 0.5%), a 6.0% medium-term growth rate, and a 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macro recovery than expected
n Share gains in the PC market
n Better-than-expected cost controls
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory cost
Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro recovery than expected
n Share loss in the PC market
n Worse-than-expected cost controls
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory cost
Asustek Computer Inc. (2357.TW)
Base case, sum of the parts:

We apply a 9x multiple to Asustek's 2026 brand profits. This is somewhat below the midpoint
of its historical band of 7-15x since 2009, which we view as fair because memory prices are
rising and will have a negative impact on margins.
We value its stakes in Pegatron and Advantech based on our price targets in proportion to
Asustek's holdings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 094: 260812_ubs_MSI

Source PDF: all report pdf/260812_ubs_MSI.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 18
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 12 August 2026
Global Research
Micro-Star International
Navigating component headwinds better than
expected
Q226 margin surprisingly held up amid  component cost pressure
MSI reported Q2 sales NT$49.9bn, -11.3% QoQ/-18.6% YoY and below consensus
expectation of 5% sequential decline, due to softer demand as consumers awaits for
better entry point on intensive price hikes. This was in line with our view on slower pull-
ins from higher component costs. That being said, margins held up better than

expected. Gross margin improved 1ppt QoQ to 16.0%, ahead of UBSe/Street's forecasts
of 12.2%/12.8%; while OpM declined slightly from 6.9% in Q1 to 6.6% in Q2. We have
underestimated its  flexibility on material procurement, inventory management, and
pricing adjustments, which had led to sustainable margins. Q2 EPS was NT$3.55,
42%/49% higher than UBSe/Street thanks to better margins. July sales were reported at
NT$19.3bn, +8.9% MoM & flat YoY, suggesting some shipment recovery.

Favourable pricing uplifts from graphic card price hikes and better product mix
Our studies suggest more hefty graphic card price hikes  are likely to hit the market in
H226 as major chipset suppliers seek to pass on higher component cost increases. Mid
to high-end graphic cards, such as Nvidia RTX 50-series retail pricing are looking to surge
by 20-40% as strong AI server demand are taking up most GDDR memory supplies,
leading to intensive price increases for consumer graphic cards. While we anticipate

memory price hikes to somewhat weighing on consumer demand, MB/VGA suppliers
have been showing optimism on MB/VGA shipments into H226E as AI applications drive
device/equipment upgrades with potential demand uptick for holiday seasons. The
industry is taking on rising risks of component price hikes, notably for memory and SSD,
where MB/VGA has been more proactively adjusting selling prices to secure reasonable
profitability. Meanwhile, Micro-Star has noted its first batch of Nvidia N1X chipset

shipment   is seeing strong demand with increasing orders from US/China customers.
MSI's RTX Spark PC is scheduled to launch by end of Q3 and early Q426; and AI server
products are also going through customer qualifications. We project sales to grow 10%
and 4% in Q326E and Q426E, respectively, mainly driven by better graphic card pricing
and better product mix for higher value added products including AI PCs and AI servers.
Valuation: Refresh PT to NT$155 and maintain Neutral

We  lift 2027/2028E sales growth from 7%/9% YoY to 11%/10% YoY mainly to reflect
higher pricing (despite slower volumes) as AI demand will likely continue to drive GPU
price hikes, especially for high-end graphic cards. We raise 2026/2027/2028E EPS by
15%/17%/17%, and lift PT from NT$108 to NT$155, based on 13x 2028E P/E (rolled
over from 12x 2027 P/E), in line with global hardware peers and implies 0.8x PEG.  While
we are seeing improving product mix, MSI stock has gone up 58% since end of Apr,

outperforming its Taiwanese MB/VGA peers Asustek/Gigabyte +44%/+28%.  We see
balanced risk-reward and fairly priced with limited AI exposure, especially for servers.

### Page 2

Micro-Star International UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q:   Will graphics cards sales resume growth?
Likely. Previous UBS Evidence Lab studies showed pricing for NVDA RTX 50 series plateaued in 2025
after its launch, yet we recently has started to see retail market data suggesting pricing is picking up
again for consumer graphic cards.  Mid to high-end graphic cards are looking to surge by 20-40% in

H226 and potentially up further into 2027/2028E as strong AI server demand are taking up most
GDDR memory supplies, leading to intensive price hikes for consumer graphic cards. While we
anticipate memory price hikes to somewhat weighing on consumer demand/volumes, we are seeing
a wider price hikes in major markets including US and China, which could lead to graphic card market
value growth in 2026E.
Q: Will MSI benefit from the rise of AI servers?

Unlikely at the moment. Although we think Taiwan is the foundation of the computing industry and
Taiwan's server system makers command roughly 90% global market share  of server MBs, including
AI servers, MSI has limited AI server exposure as a late comer. Notably, most of MSI's business is driven
by components, which we believe are still highly cyclical and impacted by consumer demand and the
macro environment. We see the company making efforts to win server projects but its server revenue

contribution remains small at below low single digits and will take time to meaningfully contribute.
We still anticipate relatively slow MB/VGA volumes as component price hikes continue to weigh on
consumer demand. UBS forecasts 2026 PC shipment at +1% YoY, holding up on the back of ASPs
+13.5% YoY as OEMs pass on higher memory and CPU costs; and project PC units +2% YoY in
2027E (LINK). We anticipate AI demand will likely continue to drive GPU price hikes, especially for

high-end graphic cards; where better graphic card pricing and better product mix toward higher
value added products including AI PCs and AI servers will be the major revenue driver while margin
sustainability remains to be proven.
Retail website (link) has shown Nvidia's RTX 50 series of Blackwell graphic cards has seen significant
price increase over the past few months, up c.40% already in August, while GeForce RTX
5060/5070/5080 are also seeing modest price increase, mainly driven by memory pricing

adjustments. Asustek and Gigabyte have yet reported Q2 results, but MSI inventories have gone up
by 25%QoQ/30% YoY with DOI up from 90 days in Q126 to 115 days in Q226, suggesting pull-ins
ahead of component price hikes and preparation for  new product launches.
The stock is trading at 14x/13x 2027/2028E P/E, vs. its historical range of 6-16x. We believe its recent
performance (MSI stock  up 58% since end of Apr, outperforming its Taiwanese MB/VGA peers

Asustek/Gigabyte +44%/+28%) has factored in the expectations on stronger consumer graphic card
pricing, but we see balanced risk-reward considering  limited AI exposure, especially for servers.

Established in 1986, MSI leads in graphics card and MB manufacturing, as well as gaming PCs. In
2024, components (including graphics, MBs and monitors)  accounted for 48% of MSI's total
revenue, followed by system products (including notebooks and desktops), at 45%, and other
products (such as enterprise platform solutions, automotive and commercial solutions), at 7%.

NT$90.00 downside +2% +5% +4% 3.3% NT$9

Source: UBS estimates
Risk to the current share price is balanced (1:1)
Micro-Star International is trading at NT$154.00 (as of 11 Aug 2026)
UPSIDE (NT$215.00):  In our upside scenario, we factor in 1) AI upcycle driving
component shortages leading to price hikes for PC and consumer graphic cards; 2)
stronger-than-expected PC and gaming demand; 3) development into new server
business momentum exceeding expectations; and 4) stronger margins from a favourable

product mix and better operating leverage. We assume 2028E total revenue of NT
$293.0bn, with components and systems to grow at 18% and 14% YoY in 2028E,
respectively, and we assume net margin of 4.1% to arrive at 2028E EPS of $14.10. We
then apply ~15x multiple to 2028 EPS to arrive at $215 upside valuation.
BASE (NT$155.00): We assume 2028E total revenue of NT$266.3bn with components
and systems to grow at 10% and 10% YoY in 2028E, respectively, and we assume net

margin of 3.8% to arrive at 2028E EPS of $12.03. We then apply ~13x multiple to 2028E
EPS to arrive at $155 price target.
DOWNSIDE (NT$90.00): In our downside scenario, we factor in worse-than-expected
PC and gaming demand, a lack of new server business development and weaker margins
from less operating leverage and intensified competition. We assume 2028E total
revenue of NT$$233.4bn with components and systems to grow at 2% and 5% YoY in
2028E, respectively, and we assume net margin of 3.3% to arrive at 2028E EPS of $9.16.

We then apply ~10x multiple to 2028E EPS to arrive at $90 downside valuation.

Company Description
Established in 1986, Micro-Star International (MSI) leads in graphics card and motherboard
manufacturing, as well as gaming PCs. In 2024, components (including graphics,
motherboards and monitors) accounted for 48% of MSI's total revenue, followed by system
products (including notebooks and desktops), at 45%, and other products (such as enterprise

platform solutions, automotive and commercial solutions), at 7%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for Micro-Star International (MSI) is based on P/E ratio.
Company-specific downside risks include: 1) weak demand for graphic processors (GPUs) and
gaming PC leading to softer graphic card sales; 2) a weak PC market leading to a slow
motherboard replacement cycle; and 3) intensified margin pressure on pricing competition or
an inventory correction.

Upside risks include: 1) stronger-than-expected demand for GPUs and gaming PCs leading to
substantial growth in graphic card sales; 2) a strong PC market leading to an accelerated
motherboard replacement cycle; and 3) clearer evidence of a structurally sustainable
AI server business supporting a valuation re-rating.
Sector risks: Investing in the technology sector involves a high degree of risk. Rapid
technological changes, increasing competition and exposure to macroeconomic cycles are

among the many risks faced by investors in technology stocks. Moreover, it is extremely
difficult to project the financial results of tech companies, since their operating models are
highly volatile and unpredictable. Finally, valuing technology stocks can prove challenging, as
neither traditional nor non-traditional valuation measures provide much insight into how
these stocks trade.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Overall July ODM notebook shipments came in 4% below our estimate:
Component constraints remain a bottleneck, but there is evidence of a fade in the 1H
pull-forward demand. However, we do expect product mix to continue to shift
toward higher-end, more-profitable models. On their recent 2Q earnings calls, the

majority of ODMs noted  that 2H NB builds will be weaker than 1H, which is atypical
of historical seasonality (historically NB builds are 2H>1H). We forecast August NB
builds at 9.0mn (+10% q/q, -20% y/y).
We lower our 3Q notebook build estimate by 6% to 27.3mn units (-10% q/q, -
20% y/y), after factoring in latest guidance from the ODMs post 2Q earnings call.
This 10% sequential decline is sub-seasonal vs. the 15-year average seasonality of
+5% q/q in 3Q. Actual shipments will remain reliant on component availability, but

we continue to hear about constraints and expect this to continue to cap low-end
notebook model builds. Quanta commented on its recent earnings call that 2Q is
typically the peak season for Chromebooks, but due to Chromebook's target market
being more price sensitive, as well as component constraints, Chromebook demand
this year in 2Q was sub-seasonal, accounting for <10% of total notebooks builds
during the quarter.

Key Takeaways
Top five ODMs' July 2026 NB shipments were 8.2mn units (-31% m/m, -24% y/y),
4% below our estimate.
We estimate August 2026 NB builds at 9.0mn units (+10% m/m, -20% y/y).
We lower our 3Q26 NB build estimate by 6% to 27.3mn units (-10% q/q, -20% y/
y).

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 3

Alchip (3661.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Existing Customer Secured; Next Major CSP Opportunity Emerging  — Alchip
believes its existing CSP relationship is increasingly sticky after multiple
generations of cooperation, with the customer already assigning it the next few
generational designs and architectures. Customer concentration nevertheless
remains high, and management acknowledged that meaningful diversification
requires winning another major hyperscaler, we believe is Google as it is the biggest

AI ASIC customer with large volume.  As CSPs increasingly adopt COT and multi-
vendor sourcing, Alchip expects additional opportunities across compute-die
design, KGD and packaging integration, supporting management’s expectation for
AI-related growth broadly in line with key customers through 2029.
Maintaining Buy, TP NT$6,800 — Given higher ASP , more value added from Alchip
and also remaining strong capex expansion from its major CSP customers, we lift

our earnings projection for Alchip by 11%/15%/32% for 2026/2027 /2028. We
maintain Buy on Alchip with higher target price NT$6,800 (from NT$6,200), based
on 35x of our 2027 EPS estimate. We lower our target PER from 37x to 35x to
reflect recent market uncertainties.

### Page 7

Alchip (3661.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
7
Alchip
Company description
Alchip is a leading provider of silicon design and manufacturing service,
specializing in ASIC (Application-Specific Integrated Circuit) design,
development, and production. Founded in 2003, the company has >1,000
employees and operates in Taipei, Shanghai, Japan, Korea, and the US. Alchip
serves a wide range of industries, including automotive, industrials, consumer
electronics, and computing.

Investment strategy
We rate Alchip as Buy. We expect Alchip to deliver a strong snapback in
2026/27 with its N3 project ramping up. Moreover, we are also positive over
the long-term growth opportunity from its AI ASIC projects with its major CSP
client. Alchip remains one of the few design service providers within TSMC’s
3DFabric alliance. In our view, if CSPs work more aggressively on their internal
design and become more competitive on product offerings, Alchip would be

well positioned as a back-end/turnkey partner.
Valuation
Our target price for Alchip of NT$6,800 is based on 35x 2027E EPS. 35x
target PER is largely in line with its historical 3-year PER range, which we see
as justified on account of strong growth potential of its AI ASIC projects with
its key CSP client, with better project cycle and strong growth momentum
resuming next year. Our target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 11x/8x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected NRE

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

## Document 111: 260814_daiwa_Gigabyte

Source PDF: all report pdf/260814_daiwa_Gigabyte.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-14
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Gigabyte released its 2Q26 results after market hours on 14 August. Gigabyte’s 
2Q26 earnings came in at TWD6.6bn (EPS: TWD9.9), which was 42% and 19% higher 
than our and the street’s expectations, respectively. 2Q26 sales came in at TWD144.2bn 
(up 37% QoQ and 41% YoY), 16% higher than our estimate on better-than-expected 
enterprise AI server demand. However, gross margin decreased 2.4pp QoQ to 9.6%, 0.6pp 
lower than the street’s expectation, mainly due to a higher-than-expected contribution from 
AI server revenue. Operating margin similarly dropped 1.4pp QoQ to 5.6%, following the 
gross margin trend but partially offset by the elevated operating leverage on its expanded 
top line. Net profit was boosted by a higher-than-expected FX gain of TWD885m. Looking 
into 3Q26, we expect the revenue contribution from Neo Cloud clients to become more 
significant, and therefore we believe the top line will continue to grow through 3Q26. 
Moving into 2027, we anticipate Neo Cloud demand will strengthen further, driven by 
increased procurement of Nvidia AI servers from these clients. Lastly, the company 
elevated its 2026 capex outlook from TWD2.3-2.5bn to TWD2.8-3.0bn. To support this, 
Gigabyte has announced the issuance of 40-50m new shares, implying a dilution of c.7-
7.5%. We are currently reviewing our model and will provide an update in due course. We 
have a Buy (1) rating on Gigabyte with a 12M TP of TWD422, based on a target PER of 
15x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key risk: earnings impact from Nvidia’s 
business model change.

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take from Gigabyte’s 2Q26 results. We are currently reviewing our model 
and will provide an update in due course.

• 2Q26 results beat on sales and FX gain. Gigabyte’s 2Q26 earnings came in at 
TWD6.6bn (EPS: TWD9.9), which was 42% and 19% higher than our and the street’s 
expectations, respectively. 2Q26 sales came in at TWD144.2bn (up 37% QoQ and 41% 
YoY), 16% higher than our estimate on better-than-expected enterprise AI server 
demand. However, gross margin decreased 2.4pp QoQ to 9.6%, 0.6pp lower than the 
street’s expectation, mainly due to a higher-than-expected contribution from AI server 
revenue. Operating margin similarly dropped 1.4pp QoQ to 5.6%, following the gross 
margin trend but partially offset by the elevated operating leverage on its expanded top 
line. Net profit was boosted by a higher-than-expected FX gain of TWD885m.

Gigabyte: monthly revenue trend and YoY growth

Source: Company

• Business outlook. Gigabyte reported July sales at TWD44.2bn (up 3% MoM and 60% 
YoY), which achieved 34% of our 3Q26 sales forecast (TWD131.2bn; down 9% QoQ but 
up 65% YoY) and 35% of the Bloomberg consensus of TWD127.7bn. With July revenue 
accounting for 23-35% of Gigabyte’s 3Q revenue over the past five years, the run-rate is 
tracking ahead of our expectation. Looking into 3Q26, we expect the revenue 
contribution from Neo Cloud clients to become more significant, and therefore we 
believe the top line will continue to grow through 3Q26. Moving into 2027, we anticipate 
Neo Cloud demand will strengthen further, driven by increased procurement of Nvidia AI 
servers from these clients. Lastly, the company elevated its 2026 capex outlook from 
TWD2.3-2.5bn to TWD2.8-3.0bn. To support this, Gigabyte has announced the issuance 
of 40-50m new shares, implying a dilution of c.7-7.5%. We are currently reviewing our 
model and will provide an update in due course.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Gigabyte with a 12M TP of TWD422, based on a target PER 
of 15x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key risk: earnings impact from Nvidia’s 
business model change.

Giga-Byte Technology Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
OpM slightly below MSe but
above the Street
2Q26 result summary:
• GM was 9.6% (-240bps q/q, +20bps y/y), 60bps below MSe and 60bps
below the Street. Sequential GM decline was driven by mix change; a higher
mix of NVL72 racks helped drive revenue to grow 37% q/q.
• OpM was 5.6% (-140bps q/q, +50bps y/y), 50bps below MSe but 20bps
above the Street; OP was NT$8.1B (+10% q/q, +53% y/y), 8% below MSe but

16% above the Street.
• Non-op income was higher at ~NT$1.3B mainly due to FX gains.
• Net profit was NT$6.6B (+26% q/q, +117% y/y) for EPS of NT$9.88, 13%
above MSe and 30% above the Street.
Giga-Byte also announced a new share issuance of 40-50M common shares for its
GDR issuance, implying 6.9% dilution, at most. The raised capital will be used for
procurement of key components in foreign currency.
Stay EW: Giga-Byte's inventory continues to climb in 2Q (+28% q/q), which we think

implies Giga-Byte expects top-line momentum to grow stronger in 2H vs 1H. Nebius
is a key customer for Giga-Byte and our checks indicate that Giga-Byte is supplying a
combination of AI servers and general servers to Nebius. We think valuation looks
fair at ~12x vs its 5Y historical trading range of ~12x, so we stay EW.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Innolux  | Asia Pacific
Sub-seasonal 2H26; Glass in
Advanced Packaging  Awaiting
More Meaningful Contribution
We maintain the view that it is still premature to accumulate
shares from the angle of glass opportunities in advanced
packaging. Stay EW.
Core business update and outlook:
• Commodity display: TV demand should be relatively stable, but earlier
restocking took place in 1H26. Meanwhile, NBs are suffering from higher
component costs.

• Non-commodity display: It sees good synergies from the Pioneer acquisition
and expects benefits to emerge in the future.
• Non Display: Demand is relatively stable and should continue to grow.
Emerging semiconductor business:
• Chip first offering should  grow shipments by double digits YoY this year.
• Continued to work with partner on TGV; likely to see adoption in 2028.
• No comment on capex or capacity plans at this stage, but it will try to keep

both under control.
• Despite the possibility of more players joining the supply chain, it is
confident about its own first-mover advantage.
Fab disposal:
• No plan for aggressive fab disposal in the future; instead, will focus more on
the production facility optimization.
Our view:
• We think the market's focus is still on glass opportunities in advanced
packaging, although we sense that the enthusiasm seems to have normalized
in the past few months, before the supply chain sees major breakthrough in

### Page 3

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Innolux (3481.TW)
Base case, 2.1x 2026e P/B. We use price/book value as our methodology because we believe
it better reflects Innolux's intrinsic value, in view of the industry's relatively cyclical nature.
Innolux has generally traded at an average of 0.6x P/B, but the stock has re-rated from the
FOPLP/advanced packaging angle, We view 2.1x as justified because we see do not see semi

contribution kicking in until 2028.
Risks to Upside
n More panel price hikes amid slower ramps of Chinese players
n Higher contribution from assembly business
n Stronger TV/IT demand recovery
n Faster progress on semi businesses
Risks to Downside
n Macro demand slowdown
n Fiercer competition from Chinese panel makers
n Faster OLED panel adoptions in large size applications
n Slower progress on semi businesses

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

神基(3005) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：PER 低估，加上明年殖利率仍偏高，下方風險有
限

一、2Q26 毛利率 、 營益率略優於預期 ， 本業達成率100%，
業外淨收入挹注 ， 獲利達成率101%， 單季稅後EPS 2.52
元，累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 4.26 元 
神基 2Q26 營收 115 億元、+24%QoQ、+17%YoY，毛
利率 31.69%(原估 31.0%)、+0.63pptsQoQ、
-1.89pptsYoY，營收顯著季增，強固型 NB 比重增，惟
毛利率受累產品漲價幅度落後關鍵原物料漲，致毛利率
水準未顯著回升，營業利益 1.36 億元、+54%QoQ、
+10%YoY， 達成率101%， 營益率16.82%(原 16.6%)、
+3.31pptsQoQ、-1.19pptsYoY，費用率 14.87%、
-2.67pptsQoQ、-0.7pptsYoY，業外淨收入 1.08 億元(原
估 0.85 億元)，稅前淨利 20.44 億元、+47%QoQ、
+11%YoY，達成率 102%，稅後淨利 15.73 億元、
+45%QoQ、+12%YoY，獲利達成率 101%，單季稅後
EPS 2.52 元(原估 2.5 元)。累計 1H26 營收 208 億元、
+8%YoY，營業利益 31.94 億元、持平 YoY，稅前淨利
34.35 億元 、 持平YoY， 稅後淨利26.6 億元 、+1%YoY，
稅後 EPS 4.26 元。

二、2Q26 年三大產品線同步季增雙位數，其中又以航
太扣件為最，電子產品次之，最後是機構產品 
2Q26 營收比重，電子產品占 54%(其中四成來自國防、
車用占雙位數 、 公共事業占高個位數 ， 餘有警消 、 能源、
零售等)、+36%QoQ、+26%YoY，機構產品占 34%(含
綜合機構產品及汽車機構產品約 1/4)、+11%QoQ、
+1%YoY，航太扣件(豐達科技)占 12%、+14%QoQ、
+40%YoY。三大產品均有雙位數季成長，尤以電子產品
為最，航太扣件次之，機構產品亦有低雙位數成長。 
累計 1H26 年營收比重，電子產品占52%、+14%YoY，
機構產品占 36%、-7%YoY， 航太扣件占12%、+35%YoY，
三大產品線以電子產品及航太扣件年增雙位數為最，餘
機構產品衰退幅度逐步收斂。 
三、2026 年資本支出約 8~12 億元，其中主要以豐達科
擴產為主，餘 3~5 億元，擴產桃園龜山製造基地 
神基 2023 年資本支出達 5-7 億元，2024 年資本支出達
14.6 億元。2025 年資本支出 18 億元，其中神基 9 億多
購買設備及越南廠房，餘 9 億元來自豐達科的馬來西亞
廠及設備等支出。2026 年資本支出預將落在 8~12 億元
左右，其中豐達科資本支出較高，神基 3~5 億元，主要
支出是桃園龜山設立一個製造基地，整修完後擴張強固
型電腦生產製造，Server 機架及電池封裝 BBU 亦有發
展。 
四、展望 2026 年，強固型電腦仍有雙位數成長，PC 相
關綜合機構件年衰退高個位數至低雙位數，汽車機構件
優於預期亦雙位數成長，推估營收年增 11%，稅後 EPS 
9.34 元 
展望 2026 年，整體營運方面，維持2026 年上、下半年
營收比重分別為 48~50%、52~50%的預期 ， 下半年營運
將優於上半年，全年營收成長 5%至 10%的目標可望順
利達成。其中，強固電腦全年營收仍維持成長 20%至
30%的預期，主要動能除既有軍用市場外，公用事業、
運輸及公共安全等新應用也持續拓展。其中，公司已切
入美國大型大眾運輸客戶，涵蓋巴士、火車及校車等應
用，4Q26 初也將發表新一代產品，後續動能看正向； 
與 PC 相關的綜合機構件受到 PC 產業在漲價循環下，
終端承受能力保守看待影響，全年則預估衰退 8%至
12%，目前表現仍落在原先預期區間，但下半年已出現
回溫跡象。不過神基積極拓展高附加價值應用，在碳纖
機構件方面，目前已有兩家 NB 客戶採用於高階商用機
種，預計 4Q26 開始出貨，AI 機櫃部分，神基桃園龜山
大智新廠已在準備機櫃製造所需的重型設備，產能預計
4Q26 初全面建置完成，後續動能可望逐步加溫； 
車用機構件方面，原本預期 2026 年成長 10-16%，不過
目前整體表現優於預期，預期全年成長幅度有望超標，
成長動能主要來自自動化效益展現，同時也積極布局車
用高附加價值機構件，近期切入 tier1 客戶相關車用
Domain Controller 表面處理產品，產品單價較既有產品

五、維持買進理由 
投顧預估神基 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 9.34 元
及 10.74 元，目前股價116.5 元，PER 12X 及 11X 評價
偏低，先前股價隨軍工類股退燒回檔，目前處在相對低
水準，由於每年配息率都高達有 8 成以上，推估明年配
息殖利率仍有高達 6.44%，故股價下方風險有限，故投
資建議維持買進，目標價維持 160 元。

神基 (3005 TT)  Buy

營收低谷已過，且評價偏低，給予 Buy 的投資建議  
投資建議 ： 考量公司營收低谷已過 ，2H26 營收表現將優於 1H26，
且評價偏低，給予 Buy 的投資建議。  
2Q26 營收 YOY +17.37% ，成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件：
2Q26 神基營收為 115.13 億元， QOQ +23.68% ， YOY +17.37% ，
營收成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件 ， 營收 YOY 分別為 +27%
和 +40% ，其中電子件裡面，以強固型電腦表現較為強勁，漢通
則有部分受到筆電需求下滑的影響 。 機構件營收則為 YOY -1% ，
主要是受到筆電需求下滑的影響，不過營收 QOQ 為 +9%，顯示
需求有回溫跡象。毛利率較 1Q26 增加，雖有受到原物料漲價而
影響毛利率，不過因為較高毛利的強固型電腦拉貨強勁，使得
整體毛利率逆勢成長。  
2H26 營收將呈現旺季水準，其中強固型電腦產品為主要成長動
能： 公司給予營收展望為 1H26:2H26=48~50:50~52 ， 2026 年營
收 YOY 為 5~10% ，並表示有望達高標或是優於展望。主要成長
動能為強固型電腦產品，由全球軍事預算提升所帶動，除了軍
事外，公用事業和大眾運輸也有專案在進行，故公司認為 2026
年強固型電腦營收 YOY 為 20~30% ， 有機會高於30%。漢 通2026
年營收 YOY 為 10~16% ， 主要是強固型電腦的強勁成長所帶動 ，
不過受到筆電需求下滑的影響，整體成長有受到拖累。綜合機
構件方面， 2026 年營收 YOY 為 -8~-12% ，主要是受到筆電需求
下滑的影響，不過有看到 2H26 筆電需求有較 1H26 回溫。汽車
機構件 2026 年營收為 YOY 接近 10% 的成長。毛利率方面，雖
然有受到記憶體漲價的影響，不過主要成長動能的強固型電腦
產品毛利率高於整體平均，一正一反下，預估 2026 年毛利率與
2025 年相當。綜合以上，預估 2026 年營收為 436.09 億元， YOY 
+10.55% ；稅後純益為 54.41 億元， YOY +4.10% ；稅後 EPS 8.72
元。預估 2027 年營收為 473.87 億元， YOY +8.66% ；稅後純益
為 60.50 億元， YOY +11.18% ；稅後 EPS 9.69 元。
